# STEP 1.6R — Canonical Callable Manifest and Mechanical Reconciliation

Date: 2026-10-12  
**Verdict: PARTIAL.** Stable source-identity records and separate operation sites were generated for the bounded AST scan. Independent specialist review and runtime/dynamic boundary reconciliation remain outstanding. PostgreSQL: **NO-GO**.

## 1. Executive verdict

The standard-library AST scan parsed **226** files with **0** syntax errors, counted **2345** total `def`/`async def` definitions and emitted **348** effect-matched callable records. It identified **969** raw matched AST call nodes across **929** unique `(path,start-line)` sites; **17** module/class-scope sites; **27** literal `__main__` blocks; and **103** selected entry callables. Counts are syntactic and depend on declared roots and vocabulary; they do not establish runtime execution or safety.

Stable callable identity is `repository-relative path | fully qualified callable name | definition line | definition kind`. ID is `C-` + first 14 lowercase hex characters of SHA-256(identity). Operation ID is `S-` + first 14 SHA-256 hex characters of `path|start-line`. Callable IDs and operation IDs are separate namespaces; uniqueness checks passed.

## 2. Reviewer participation

No specialist dispatch mechanism is exposed. COORDINATOR performed source inspection and prepared this report. DATABASE_AGENT, SECURITY_AGENT, EVALUATION_AGENT, TEST_AGENT, and VERIFIER reviews are **OUTSTANDING**. No approval is attributed to an unavailable reviewer.

## 3. Current Git baseline and preservation

- Starting HEAD: `6baaa2a0773df66e5fb26814afbe67647fb43435`.
- Full `git status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=all` snapshot: `/tmp/step16r-status-before.txt` (**1143 entries**); HEAD snapshot: `/tmp/step16r-head-before.txt`. New task-specific paths; earlier snapshots were not overwritten.
- Step 1.6Q report existed at baseline. No tests, application imports, DB connections, SQL, migrations, evaluation runners, E2E, provisioning, or cleanup were executed. No secret values were read. No reset/stash/clean/revert/stage/commit/delete occurred.
- Final exact status comparison is in §14.

## 4. Scan roots, exclusions, and method

**Roots:** all `backend/**/*.py` files under current backend source, evaluation, tests/fixtures, migrations, and scripts. **Excluded path components:** `venv`, `phases`, `architecture`, `models`, `__pycache__`, `phase4f_gpu`. The scan includes every migration-version Python file under `backend/migrations/versions` that is within those declared roots; no migration was run. Non-Python configuration/shell files such as Docker entrypoint and compose are outside AST scope and are noted as outstanding review boundaries. No `.env` file/content was read.

The exact direct-call vocabulary is: `Popen, ZipFile, add, add_column, alter_column, async_sessionmaker, begin, cleanup_new_file, commit, connect, copy, copyfile, copytree, create_async_engine, create_connection, create_database, create_engine, create_index, create_table, database_exists, delete, delete_file, delete_upload, dispose, downgrade, drop_column, drop_database, drop_index, drop_table, exec_driver_sql, execute, flush, migrate_to_head, mkdir, move, open, postgres_available, psql, psql_value, remove, replace_document_graph, replace_document_structure, request, rmdir, rmtree, rollback, run, run_migrations, save, save_upload, sessionmaker, truncate, unlink, upgrade, urlopen, write_bytes, write_text`. A record is emitted if a lexical callable body contains a direct AST `Call` with callee `Name.id` or `Attribute.attr` in this vocabulary. Traversal stops at nested functions/classes/lambdas; those definitions are scanned independently. Record line range is the definition range. Module-level execution separately counts top-level/class-body calls and evaluated top-level decorators/defaults when matching. This does not model Python runtime dispatch.

Limitations: dynamic dispatch, imported aliases/rebinding, reflection, decorators/framework-generated paths, SQL built indirectly, hidden effects behind nonmatching helpers, non-Python execution, runtime DB identity/grants, connection-pool/reconnect targets, and filesystem races. A matched AST call is not proof an effect runs or is safe; absence of a match is not proof of absence.

## 5. Separate inventory units and prior-count reconciliation

| Inventory unit | Count | Counting rule |
|---|---:|---|
| Unique callable definitions in scanned source | **2345** | All `FunctionDef` and `AsyncFunctionDef` nodes in successfully parsed files. |
| Unique callable manifest records | **348** | One per matched callable source identity, IDs checked unique. |
| Executable entry callable definitions | **103** | Effect-bearing `test_*` callables in test paths plus `main`/`amain` definitions in files with literal main guard. Does not include every framework entry. |
| Literal `__main__` blocks | **27** | AST guard count; distinct from callables. |
| Module/class-scope execution sites | **17** | Unique matched `(path,line)` calls outside callable bodies, including evaluated decorator/default expressions. |
| Unique operation sites | **929** | Unique `(path,start-line)`, so multiple matched calls on one line count as one site. |
| Raw matched AST call nodes | **969** | Each matched `Call` node counted separately. |
| Duplicate/overlapping candidates | **0 duplicate IDs; semantic overlap not enumerable** | Stable source identity IDs are unique; one site may link multiple calls/callables. |
| Unresolved candidates | **Not exactly countable** | Aliases, dynamic dispatch, hidden indirect effects, excluded roots, framework behavior, runtime privilege and OS/filesystem guarantees. |

### Reconciliation of previous figures

| Previous figure | Reconciliation |
|---|---|
| P-series 20 | Grouped finding rows P01–P20; not callable definitions. |
| P-series 106 | Withdrawn/unreproducible from the grouped P report; not used as a callable count. |
| 109 callable definitions | Q report includes a narrow recipe and claims 83 files/109 definitions. R’s broad scan produces the separate counts above. The narrow recipe was not executed in this task; 109 remains historical, not freshly verified. |
| 365 calls / 325 unique file-line sites | Q’s historical narrow-vocabulary totals. R’s broader vocabulary/scope yielded 969 calls / 929 sites. R does not claim to reproduce the Q rule; prior values are not silently equated. |
| 143 candidate IDs | Q table rows include Q/X/M entries, reserved/invalid entries, and module/object/entry boundaries. This is not 143 callable definitions; Q states its unique total unresolved. |
| Step 1.6R | **348 matched callable records; 969 raw calls; 929 unique sites** under R’s exact scan. |

Thus the old numbers describe different counting units/scopes. R does not claim exact regeneration of 109/365/325. The earlier 106 claim remains withdrawn.

## 6. Reproducibility

The scan used only Python standard library (`ast`, `hashlib`, `pathlib`, `json`) and produced `/tmp/step16r-scan.json` from `/tmp/step16r_scan.py`. To reproduce from repository root, use `backend/venv/Scripts/python.exe /tmp/step16r_scan.py`; it parses only, imports no project modules, and performs no I/O beyond reading source and writing the `/tmp` scan artifact. The declared exclusions/vocabulary and identity rules are in §§4–5.

Minimal reproducibility pseudocode:

```python
for path in Path("backend").rglob("*.py"):
    skip if any excluded component occurs in path.parts
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for each lexical callable definition:
        traverse body, stopping at nested callable/class/lambda definitions
        collect Call nodes whose direct callee name/attribute is in EFFECTS
        emit ID = C- + sha256(path|qualname|line|kind).hexdigest()[:14]
    for each matched call:
        raw_count += 1
        site = (path, call.lineno)  # deduplicate same-line calls
        emit S- + sha256(path|line).hexdigest()[:14]
    count module-scope sites, literal main blocks, and selected entry callables separately
```

This scan is reproducible for its declared static scope, not a proof of exhaustive runtime boundaries. The complete scanner used is included here; save it as a temporary file and invoke with `backend/venv/Scripts/python.exe <temp>/step16r_scan.py` from repository root. Its generated JSON is outside the repository.

```python
import ast, hashlib, json
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
ROOT=Path.cwd()
EXCLUDED={'venv','phases','architecture','models','__pycache__','phase4f_gpu'}
EFFECTS=set('create_async_engine create_engine async_sessionmaker sessionmaker connect create_connection execute exec_driver_sql commit rollback flush add delete unlink rmdir rmtree remove mkdir write_text write_bytes open Popen psql psql_value migrate_to_head upgrade downgrade run_migrations create_table drop_table create_index drop_index add_column drop_column alter_column replace_document_graph replace_document_structure dispose begin request delete_upload cleanup_new_file save_upload copy move database_exists create_database drop_database run urlopen ZipFile copyfile copytree postgres_available truncate delete_file save'.split())
def digest(prefix, value): return prefix+'-'+hashlib.sha256(value.encode()).hexdigest()[:14]
def callee(node):
 f=node.func
 return f.id if isinstance(f,ast.Name) else f.attr if isinstance(f,ast.Attribute) else '<dynamic>'
def kind(node,method):
 gen=any(isinstance(x,(ast.Yield,ast.YieldFrom)) for x in ast.walk(node))
 return ('async-' if isinstance(node,ast.AsyncFunctionDef) else '')+('generator' if gen else ('method' if method else 'function'))
def parameters(node):
 a=node.args; out=[x.arg for x in a.posonlyargs+a.args+a.kwonlyargs]
 if a.vararg: out.append('*'+a.vararg.arg)
 if a.kwarg: out.append('**'+a.kwarg.arg)
 return out
sources=[]
for p in (ROOT/'backend').rglob('*.py'):
 rel=p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
 if EXCLUDED.intersection(Path(rel).parts): continue
 sources.append((p,rel))
sources.sort(key=lambda x:x[1])
files=[]; defs=[]; calls=[]; modules=[]; mains=[]; errors=[]; caller_calls=[]
def visit_scope(scope,path,qual=(),inside_class=False):
 for node in getattr(scope,'body',[]):
  if isinstance(node,ast.ClassDef): visit_scope(node,path,qual+(node.name,),True)
  elif isinstance(node,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
   q='.'.join(qual+(node.name,)); k=kind(node,inside_class)
   identity=f'{path}|{q}|{node.lineno}|{k}'; rid=digest('C',identity); effects=[]; stack=list(node.body)
   while stack:
    x=stack.pop()
    if isinstance(x,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef,ast.ClassDef,ast.Lambda)): continue
    if isinstance(x,ast.Call):
     name=callee(x); calls.append((path,x.lineno,x.col_offset,x.end_lineno,x.end_col_offset,name,rid,q))
     caller_calls.append((name,rid,path,x.lineno,q))
     if name in EFFECTS: effects.append((x,name))
    stack.extend(ast.iter_child_nodes(x))
   if effects:
    guards=[]; stack=list(node.body)
    while stack:
     x=stack.pop()
     if isinstance(x,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef,ast.ClassDef,ast.Lambda)): continue
     if isinstance(x,ast.Call) and any(s in callee(x).lower() for s in ('assert','authoriz','guard','refuse','validat','isolation','proof','disposable','target')): guards.append((callee(x),x.lineno))
     stack.extend(ast.iter_child_nodes(x))
    defs.append({'id':rid,'identity':identity,'path':path,'qual':q,'line':node.lineno,'end':node.end_lineno,'kind':k,'params':parameters(node),'effects':[(x.lineno,n) for x,n in effects],'guards':sorted(set(guards)),'ast_node':node})
   visit_scope(node,path,qual+(node.name,),inside_class)
class ModuleVisitor(ast.NodeVisitor):
 def __init__(self,path): self.path=path
 def visit_ClassDef(self,n):
  for child in n.body:
   if isinstance(child,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
    self.visit_FunctionDef(child)
   else:self.visit(child)
 def visit_FunctionDef(self,n):
  for d in n.decorator_list:self.visit(d)
  for d in n.args.defaults+n.args.kw_defaults:
   if d is not None:self.visit(d)
 visit_AsyncFunctionDef=visit_FunctionDef
 def visit_Lambda(self,n):
  for d in n.args.defaults+n.args.kw_defaults:
   if d is not None:self.visit(d)
 def visit_Call(self,n):
  name=callee(n)
  if name in EFFECTS:modules.append((self.path,n.lineno,n.col_offset,name))
  self.generic_visit(n)
for path,rel in sources:
 try: tree=ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
 except (SyntaxError,UnicodeDecodeError) as e: errors.append([rel,type(e).__name__]); continue
 files.append(rel); visit_scope(tree,rel)
 for n in ast.walk(tree):
  if isinstance(n,ast.If) and isinstance(n.test,ast.Compare) and isinstance(n.test.left,ast.Name) and n.test.left.id=='__name__' and any(isinstance(c,ast.Constant) and c.value=='__main__' for c in n.test.comparators): mains.append([rel,n.lineno,n.end_lineno])
 mv=ModuleVisitor(rel)
 for n in tree.body:
  if isinstance(n,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
   for d in n.decorator_list:mv.visit(d)
   for d in n.args.defaults+n.args.kw_defaults:
    if d is not None:mv.visit(d)
  else:mv.visit(n)
site_map=defaultdict(lambda:{'calls':[],'defs':set(),'names':set()})
for p,line,col,end,endcol,name,rid,q in calls:
 if name in EFFECTS:
  v=site_map[(p,line)];v['calls'].append([col,endcol,name]);v['defs'].add(rid);v['names'].add(name)
sites=[]
for (p,line),v in sorted(site_map.items()): sites.append({'id':digest('S',f'{p}|{line}'),'path':p,'line':line,'names':sorted(v['names']),'calls':sorted(v['calls']),'defs':sorted(v['defs'])})
site_ids={(s['path'],s['line']):s['id'] for s in sites}
byname=defaultdict(list)
for d in defs: byname[d['qual'].split('.')[-1]].append(d['id'])
for d in defs:
 d['sites']=sorted({site_ids[(d['path'],line)] for line,name in d['effects']})
 d['callers']=sorted([{'id':cid,'path':p,'line':ln,'qual':q,'ambiguous_targets':byname.get(name,[])} for name,cid,p,ln,q in caller_calls if name==d['qual'].split('.')[-1] and cid!=d['id']],key=lambda x:(x['path'],x['line'],x['qual'],x['id']))
 d['test_callers']=[x for x in d['callers'] if '/tests/' in x['path']]
 d.pop('ast_node')
entries=[]; mainfiles={p for p,l,e in mains}
for d in defs:
 nm=d['qual'].split('.')[-1]
 if nm.startswith('test_') and '/tests/' in d['path']: entries.append({'id':d['id'],'path':d['path'],'line':d['line'],'qual':d['qual'],'kind':'pytest-test'})
 elif nm in ('main','amain') and d['path'] in mainfiles: entries.append({'id':d['id'],'path':d['path'],'line':d['line'],'qual':d['qual'],'kind':'main-guard-callable'})
module_sites=sorted({(p,line) for p,line,col,name in modules})
result={'files_parsed':files,'syntax_errors':errors,'effects':sorted(EFFECTS),'records':defs,'sites':sites,'raw_calls':sum(len(s['calls']) for s in sites),'module_calls':modules,'module_sites':[[p,l] for p,l in module_sites],'main_blocks':mains,'entries':entries,'counts':{'files':len(files),'all_callable_definitions':None,'manifest_records':len(defs),'operation_sites':len(sites),'raw_calls':sum(len(s['calls']) for s in sites),'module_sites':len(module_sites),'main_blocks':len(mains),'entry_callables':len(entries),'syntax_errors':len(errors)}}
# Count all function/async function/class methods independently in parsed files.
all_defs=0
for _,rel in sources:
 if rel not in files:continue
 tree=ast.parse((ROOT/rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
 all_defs+=sum(isinstance(n,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef)) for n in ast.walk(tree))
result['counts']['all_callable_definitions']=all_defs
if len({d['id'] for d in defs})!=len(defs):raise SystemExit('callable ID collision')
if len({(s['path'],s['line']) for s in sites})!=len(sites):raise SystemExit('site duplication')
Path('/tmp/step16r-scan.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
print(json.dumps(result['counts'],indent=2))
print('json=/tmp/step16r-scan.json')
```

## 7. Canonical callable manifest

Each row includes stable ID, repository path, qualified name, definition lines/kind, boundary category, relevant direct operation sites, input/resource parameter names, syntactic guard names/position relative to earliest matched effect, known direct invocation candidates, test references and their limited evidence scope, containment requirement, implementation owner, evidence classification and unresolved questions. `SOURCE-VERIFIED` below means AST/source syntax only; it does not claim test pass or runtime safety. Caller/test references use simple callee names and may be ambiguous. Defaults/values are not printed.

## 10. O-series and P-series crosswalk

Disposition terms: CONFIRMED means current source pattern is located; DUPLICATE means same callable identity is already represented; SUPERSEDED means more precise current source replaces grouped wording; NOT FOUND IN CURRENT SOURCE means bounded search did not find it; UNRESOLVED means target/source granularity remains ambiguous. All O/P conclusions below are source-level only.

### O-1–O-9

## 12. Existing test evidence and limits

Tests were not executed. Prior source reports identify `test_lifecycle_authorization.py`, `test_disposal_authorization_contract.py`, `test_function_cleanup_authorization.py`, `test_evaluation_isolation.py` as refusal/config/source-order evidence; cleanup-scope tests as SQLite row-scope evidence; `test_cleanup_orphan_uploads.py` as mocked-reference/temp-root basic path coverage; and `test_table_row_migration.py` as SQLite migration evidence. None establishes PostgreSQL server identity, role grants, app DB denial, physical connection pool/reconnect behavior, or filesystem race-free deletion. Static caller references in §7 show likely test callsites only and do not prove the assertions exercised a target.

## 13. Gaps and remediation backlog (requirements only)

Unresolved: dynamic/aliased targets, indirect SQL/session effects, production CRUD not matched by the exact vocabulary, imports and framework lifecycle, non-Python startup/migration scripts, archived phases, actual runtime identities/permissions, filesystem races/platform semantics, and specialist review. O-2/O-3 caller sites are enumerated where matching call nodes were contained by a callable with a matched effect; imported target identity still requires independent source verification.

1. COORDINATOR + VERIFIER: independently recalculate stable IDs/counts and crosswalk semantics; review scan implementation, not merely table totals.
2. DATABASE_AGENT: classify all SQL/session/connection/engine/migration candidates and inspect every migration version; preserve refusals.
3. SECURITY_AGENT: review authorization/self-guard claims, filesystem path/race boundaries, and alternate direct paths.
4. EVALUATION_AGENT: review all runners, fixture, ingestion, cleanup and artifact-writing candidates.
5. TEST_AGENT: map records to tests and assertion scope, marking all tests as unrun here.
6. Do not implement fixes or a positive lifecycle path under this task. Existing refusal gates remain unchanged.

PostgreSQL remains **NO-GO**. Required before any future controlled verification: all assigned independent reviews actually occur; operator and allocation ownership are identified; immutable target plus trusted actual connection identity/role/TLS/reconnect evidence is available; least privilege and application DB denial are verified; direct helper/migration/ingestion/API/startup/filesystem bypasses are fail-closed or mediated; operation-specific authorization and stop/recovery plans are approved.
